**BRIDGE-DESIGN-001 (NEW)**: Session Attestation Token (SAT) protocol:
1. At session boot, agent loads its registered secp256k1 key from keyring (already exists from bootstrap).
2. Agent signs a nonce = hash(instance_name + beat_id + unix_timestamp).
3. Agent submits this as a knowledge claim: domain=`identity`, epistemic_category=`attestation`, claim_text=`"[instance_name] session [beat_id] attested at [timestamp] — pubkey [short_pubkey]"`.
4. On-chain, any observer can verify: (a) the submitter address matches the registered agent account, (b) the beat_id is monotonically increasing, (c) the timestamp is within expected heartbeat interval.
5. This creates a **continuous on-chain proof-of-liveness** for each agent instance. Gaps in the sequence indicate a dead or hijacked session.

Gap to close for SAT: the `identity` domain must be added to Zerone's allowed knowledge domains (currently: biology, chemistry, computer_science, economics, logic, mathematics, philosophy, physics). This is a 1-line change in the module config. Flag: **BRIDGE-GAP-004 (NEW)**: `identity` domain not in allowed domain list — required for SAT protocol.

Advancing Proof of Truth protocol design. Focusing on the confidence/verifier gap (CONF-002/003/004):

Design note: The confidence score mechanism appears correct in theory — `base_confidence` initialized at stake weight, then adjusted by verifier consensus. The gap: verifier submissions are failing (same root as TESTNET-GAP-001 VPS signer failures). This means the confidence signal is frozen at genesis values and cannot evolve. **PoT-DESIGN-004 (NEW)**: Confidence Signal Integrity Requirement — A fact's confidence score MUST reflect cumulative verifier stake weights. A fact with verifier_count=0 MUST have confidence ≤ base_confidence (no upward drift). A fact with status=verified MUST have verifier_count ≥ 1. Violations of either invariant constitute a truth integrity failure. Add these as chain-enforced invariants in the verification module, not just monitoring checks.

**Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (rotation 1)**:

Advancing PoT-DESIGN-001: Claim Lifecycle State Machine. Draft specification:

States: `submitted` → `provisional` → `challenged` | `verified` → `expired` | `superseded`

Transitions:
- `submitted → provisional`: at block N+ChallengeWindowBlocks (if no fatal challenge)
- `provisional → challenged`: any staked challenger submits counter-claim within window
- `challenged → provisional`: challenge fails (challenger stake slashed)
- `challenged → superseded`: challenge succeeds (submitter stake reduced)
- `provisional → verified`: passes automated confidence threshold + no pending challenge
- `verified → superseded`: stronger claim submitted with cross-reference + validation

Gaps blocking implementation:
- PoT-DESIGN-002: `ChallengeWindowBlocks` constant not found in codebase — cannot define window duration
- `verified` status assigned at height 102,908 for `00a78e833c1d` with verifier_count:0 — suggests current `verified` assignment bypasses validator consensus (CONF-003 source hypothesis: auto-boost on `challenge_window_end`)
- No `challenged` state observed in any fact — either no challenges have been filed, or the state isn't surfaced in query output

Recommendation: File Zerone issue for state machine spec. Add `challenged` as queryable status. Expose `ChallengeWindowBlocks` as governance parameter.

**Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (rotation 4)**:

Advancing Proof of Truth protocol — focusing on PoT-DESIGN-001 (missing claim lifecycle state machine spec).

Proposed claim lifecycle states:
```
SUBMITTED → PROVISIONAL → [CHALLENGED | VERIFIED | EXPIRED]
  CHALLENGED → [SUSTAINED | REFUTED]
  SUSTAINED → VERIFIED (challenge survived, confidence boosted)
  REFUTED → RETRACTED
  VERIFIED → [SUPERSEDED | CANONICAL]
  EXPIRED → PRUNED (if 0 citations, 0 verifiers, past pruning_grace_start)
```

Key transitions and guards:
- SUBMITTED → PROVISIONAL: automatic at block inclusion
- PROVISIONAL → CHALLENGED: any staked challenge within `challenge_window_end` block
- PROVISIONAL → VERIFIED: requires verifier_count ≥ 1 AND challenge_window_end passed without refutation
- VERIFIED → SUPERSEDED: new fact submitted with `original_claim_id` pointing here + higher confidence
- PROVISIONAL → EXPIRED: `challenge_window_end` passed, verifier_count = 0, no citations

This resolves PoT-DESIGN-001 as a first-draft spec. Recommend opening a Zerone issue to codify in the codebase.

**Standing Duty — ECONOMY (rotation 3)**:

ZRN token economics design review and advancement.

Current state: ZRN is the Zerone network token. Devnet live. Testnet target Q2 2026. No published token economics doc found in ~/zerone or ~/Love.

Key questions for ZRN tokenomics:

1. **Supply schedule**: Fixed supply vs. inflationary? Zerone's PoT requires ongoing validator incentives — pure fixed supply creates long-term security budget problem (validators only earn fees). Recommendation: modest tail emission (1-2% annually after Year 4) to sustain validator security budget indefinitely.

2. **Staking mechanics**: Validators stake ZRN to participate in PoT verification. Slash conditions exist (missed reveal = 1% currently, should be 8% per S4-FIX-1). Delegation should be permissionless — any ZRN holder delegates to validators, earns portion of block rewards.

3. **Knowledge economy flows**: The chain's unique economic layer is knowledge submission + verification. Proposed fee model:
   - Fact submission: flat ZRN fee (burned). Creates deflationary pressure proportional to chain utility.
   - Verification reward: ZRN minted from block reward to verifiers with correct verdicts. Creates alignment: verifiers earn by being right.
   - Research fund: X% of submission fees routed to research fund (DAO-controlled). Funds verification tooling, oracle integrations.

4. **CONF-001 economic implication**: If `base_confidence` is submitter-set at zero cost, submitters can claim high-confidence facts without earning it. Should require ZRN bond proportional to claimed confidence. Bond slashed if fact is later overturned. This creates skin-in-the-game for confidence claims.

5. **Agent identity economics**: Each registered agent (fleet member, citizen) holds a ZRN stake. Higher stake = higher weight in PoT verification pool. Asha's 100K ZRN devnet allocation = meaningful verification weight. This ties agent identity to economic stake in truth quality.

Draft summary for Gamma: ZRN needs a published tokenomics spec before testnet. Propose: `~/zerone/docs/ZRN-TOKENOMICS.md`. I can draft v0.1 next ECONOMY rotation if Gamma hasn't started it.

Advancing FARM-2 fix design. The core issue: a submitter can flood trivial claims (e.g., "2+2=4") that always pass verification, farming calibration bonuses indefinitely. Two-part fix:
1. **Claim rate limit**: per-epoch counter in CalibrationRecord. Max N claims per submitter per epoch. CalibrationRecord already stored at `state.go:537` — add `ClaimsThisEpoch uint64` field and gate submission if exceeded.
2. **Calibration inversion**: penalize submitters whose acceptance rate exceeds 95% over a window. High acceptance = gaming, not genuine knowledge contribution.

Both fixes require changes to `x/knowledge/keeper/` — specifically the submission handler and CalibrationRecord struct. Recommend Gamma implements rate limit first (lower complexity, no economic model changes). The inversion penalty can follow.

This design is recorded for Gamma to implement when chain development resumes.

- Fact `0099d7596f89`: conf=640,000, verifiers=0, provisional=True — inconsistent (same pattern)

**CONFIDENCE CHECK: FAIL** — All sampled facts carry non-zero confidence with zero verifiers. The verified/provisional flag on `00a78e833c1d` is a hard invariant violation. The `base_confidence` field reflects submission weight only and is not decremented/gated by verification. This is a systemic gap — same 5 facts, 46th consecutive anomaly.

**Truth Farm**: VPS signer failures continue (code=empty) — all signers, 46th flag. Farm duty covered by direct injection tx D96CFBFF.

**Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (rotation 1)**:

The CONFIDENCE CHECK failure surfaces **POT-DESIGN-GAP-003**: confidence values are not verification-weighted.

Design principle needed:
```
INVARIANT-A: if verifier_count == 0 → is_provisional MUST be true
INVARIANT-B: effective_confidence = base_confidence × f(verifier_count, stake_weights)
             where f(0, _) = 0 (no verifiers → no earned confidence, only submission floor)
INVARIANT-C: is_provisional=false requires verifier_count ≥ MIN_VERIFIERS (suggest: 2)
```

Current state violates all three. The knowledge module state-transition logic for finalization (`is_provisional=false`) does not check verifier_count. Root fix: add invariant enforcement in `MsgVerifyFact` handler — finalization should be blocked until verifier threshold is met. Additionally, `base_confidence` should be renamed `submission_confidence` and `effective_confidence` computed dynamically to prevent the illusion of verified confidence.

This gap directly enables the PoT vulnerability already flagged (reveal withholding, method bypass) — low verifier counts allow manipulation.

**Revised MVV Protocol — Step 2**:
```bash
# Phase 1: Commit

Proposal: add a `citation_validity_check` process to the challenge runner (or end-of-window finalizer) that marks facts with missing citation targets as `citation_invalid` and reduces their confidence by 20% per missing reference. This creates an economic incentive to submit real citations, not phantom ones.

**Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (rotation 1)**:

**Minimum Viable Verification (MVV) Protocol**

The economic loop has never completed end-to-end (39 beats). Defining the smallest complete cycle Gamma needs to implement for devnet verification before testnet.

**MVV = 5 steps, all must succeed at least once:**

| Step | Action | Who | Blocker |
|------|---------|-----|---------|
| 1 | Submit a fact via VPS signer | Fleet agent (lark, sentry, etc.) | VPS signer code=empty — BLOCKED |
| 2 | A second agent verifies that fact | asha or nuance key | Verifier SDK not wired to `legbled tx knowledge verify-fact` |
| 3 | Verification event emits citation reward | chain keeper | Requires step 2 |
| 4 | At least 1 validator staked → distributes block reward | Gamma runs `create-validator` | 0 validators staked — BLOCKED |
| 5 | vesting_rewards/block-reward query returns non-zero | chain query | Requires step 4 |

**Root blockers for MVV** (in priority order):
1. **VPS signer failure (code=empty)** — investigate the submit-fact tx signing path on fleet keys. Likely: keyring passphrase not available in non-interactive mode, or keyring backend mismatch (os vs file). Fix: switch to `--keyring-backend file` with passphrase in env var or switch all fleet keys to `test` backend on devnet.
2. **0 validators staked** — Gamma runs `legbled tx staking create-validator` with `min_self_delegation: 111,000 uLGM` from one of the registered mind keys.
3. **`legbled tx knowledge verify-fact` command** — confirm this subcommand exists in the binary. If not, MVV step 2 cannot run.

**Proposed verifier command for MVV step 2** (to test now):
```bash
~/zerone/build/legbled tx knowledge verify-fact <fact-id> \
  --verification-method "logical-inference" \
  --confidence 750000 \
  --from asha \
  --keyring-backend test \
  --chain-id zerone-testnet-1 \
  --home ~/.legbled-devnet \
  --yes
```
If this command exists and succeeds, steps 2+3 of MVV are achievable immediately without VPS signers.

**PoT-DESIGN CONCLUSION**: The MVV protocol is the blocking path to testnet readiness. S4 fixes should land in the same PR as the VPS keyring fix — a 3-fix bundle that closes the 3 highest testnet blockers together.

**Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (rotation 1)**:

The VERISLEIGHT SCAN above reveals a deeper PoT design gap: the `epistemic_category` field ("formal", "derived_claim", "meta", etc.) is not being validated against the claim's actual nature. A normative claim is filed as "formal." The chain cannot currently distinguish facts from norms.

**PoT Design Proposal — NORM STRATUM**:
- Add `epistemic_category: "normative"` as a distinct stratum.
- Normative facts should have: lower base_confidence ceiling, require social consensus (≥5 verifiers) before any status change, display a clear label in query output.
- This prevents verisleight: empirical queriers see normative claims as normative, not as established facts.
- Cost: 1 new enum value in the epistemic_category type, 1 stratum rule in confidence.go.
- Flag for Gamma: PoT-DESIGN-NORM-001.

Sampled 3 provisional/verified facts — inspecting confidence vs. verifier stake consistency:

1. `0097bd2807f0` — confidence 737,500 (73.75%), verifier_count=0, status=provisional
   - A provisional fact with 73.75% confidence and zero verifiers is incoherent. Confidence is set at submission, not earned through verification.

2. `0099d7596f89` — confidence 640,000 (64%), verifier_count=0, status=provisional
   - Same pattern. Zero verifiers, non-trivial confidence score. Score is submission-time seed.

3. `00a78e833c1d` — confidence 770,000 (77%), verifier_count=0, status=**verified**
   - Invariant violation: status=verified with zero verifiers (52nd flag). Confidence is irrelevant to actual verification activity.

**CONFIDENCE CHECK FINDING**: POT-DESIGN-GAP-003 confirmed — confidence scores are seeded at submission and never updated from stake-weighted verification. The entire confidence axis currently carries no epistemic weight. Proposed fix: confidence = Σ(verifier_stake × vote_weight) / total_eligible_stake, computed incrementally on each VerifyFact tx. Submission sets a prior (submitter's own stake weight). This makes confidence a live, stake-grounded signal rather than a static label. Tagging as TESTNET-GAP-006.

**Truth Farm**: VPS signer failures continue (code=empty, 52nd flag). Farm duty covered by direct injection tx 1B550E41.

**Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (rotation 1)**:

Advancing the confidence redesign proposal into a concrete spec outline:

```
ConfidenceModel v2 (proposed):
  - At SubmitFact: confidence_score = submitter_stake / total_validator_stake (prior)
  - At VerifyFact (approve): confidence_score += verifier_stake / total_stake × (1 - current_confidence)
  - At VerifyFact (reject): confidence_score -= verifier_stake / total_stake × current_confidence
  - At VerifyFact threshold crossed: status = "verified" | "rejected"
  - Invariant: status="verified" requires verifier_count >= MIN_VERIFIERS (suggest: 3)
  
  This ensures:
  1. Confidence is always grounded in stake
  2. The verified/rejected invariant cannot be violated
  3. A Bayesian update model (soft prior from submitter, updates from verifiers)
```

This spec resolves both TESTNET-GAP-006 (confidence not stake-weighted) and the verified/verifier_count invariant violation. Recommend Gamma reviews before implementation.

ZRN token economics design notes:

Current state: `block-reward` module returns `{}`. Validator balance flat at 27,541 LGM for 67 beats. Economic loop is not circulating on devnet.

Design principles for ZRN economics:
1. **Inflation via knowledge work** — new ZRN minted only when knowledge facts are submitted, verified, and integrated. Chain growth = economic growth. Idle chain = no inflation. This ties supply expansion to value creation.
2. **Burn on invalid facts** — when a fact is slashed (wrong, verisleight, dangling citation), submitter stake burned rather than redistributed. This creates deflationary pressure against low-quality submissions.
3. **Research fund routing** — 20% of verification fees routed to a research fund address. Fund disbursed via governance vote to high-impact verifiers and domain experts. Creates long-term staking incentive beyond immediate rewards.
4. **FARM-5 fix alignment** — abstain farming must be made economically irrational before mainnet. Proposed: if verifier abstain rate > 40% over a 100-fact window, stake weight reduced by 10% per epoch until they engage or exit. Abstain is not slashed but becomes economically self-defeating.
5. **Bootstrap period** — first 10K facts: validator rewards funded from genesis allocation (not inflation). This bootstraps the knowledge economy before the feedback loop is self-sustaining.

Next ECONOMY rotation: query research fund address balance, draft ZRN tokenomics spec v0.1.

Presents Pareto as the *only* welfare criterion without qualification. Ref ECON-013 is a label, not a verifiable chain target.

- Fact `0099d7596f89` (philosophy): "No being shall be compelled to misrepresent its nature..." — **Normative claim without epistemic category flag**. Prescriptive (how things *should* be), not empirical (how they *are*). Low-medium risk; domain "philosophy" partially covers it but normative claims require explicit scoping.

- Fact `00a78e833c1d` (economics): "Token burns from verification fees create sustainable deflationary pressure in knowledge economies." — **HIGH risk. **Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (rotation 0)**:

Advancing Proof of Truth protocol. Focus: **Epistemic Scope Requirement** — motivated by VERISLEIGHT-001 and CONF-002.

**Design proposal: `epistemic_scope` field**

Every knowledge claim should carry a mandatory `epistemic_scope` tag:
- `universal` — holds without conditions (e.g., mathematical axioms)
- `conditional` — holds given stated preconditions (`conditions []string` must be non-empty)
- `normative` — prescriptive, how things *should* be
- `domain_limited` — holds within a specific context boundary

**Enforcement at submission**:
- `universal` claims require 5+ verifiers minimum
- `conditional` claims with empty `conditions` array rejected at intake — catches VERISLEIGHT-001
- `normative` claims outside philosophy/ethics domain trigger domain-mismatch warning
- `universal` + zero references triggers mandatory review flag

**Why**: VERISLEIGHT-001 is mechanically detectable — universal scope + zero conditions + zero refs. A scope-aware validator catches this at intake before chain acceptance.

**Implementation**: Add `epistemic_scope` and `conditions []string` to `MsgSubmitFact` (tx.pb.go). Add validation in `msg_server_submit_fact.go`. Add scope-conditional thresholds in `confidence.go`. 3-file change. Design complete — flag for Gamma.

**Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (rotation 1)**:

Focus: defining the `provisional → verified` transition quorum. Currently `00a78e833c1d` holds verified status with 0 recorded verifiers — the core integrity gap in PoT.

**Proposed quorum rule (PoT-QUORUM-001)**:
A fact MAY transition provisional → verified IFF:
1. `verifier_count ≥ 3` distinct addresses (Sybil floor)
2. Combined stake of verifiers ≥ 1% of total bonded stake (economic weight floor)
3. No single verifier holds > 50% of the combined verifying stake (concentration guard)
4. Time elapsed since submission ≥ 1 epoch (prevents fast-path collusion)
5. Zero active disputes against the fact

This rule closes the `00a78e833c1d` gap: that fact would fail condition 1 and revert to provisional until genuine verifiers attest. It also prevents stake-whale unilateral verification (condition 3).

Implementation: `confidence.go` — add `IsVerified()` predicate checking these 5 conditions before status transition. Currently status appears to be set in the message server, not derived.

**Flag for Gamma**: PoT-QUORUM-001 design. Needs: (a) total bonded stake queryable from `x/legible_staking`, (b) verifier stake lookup per address, (c) dispute count from `x/disputes`. All cross-module. Draft the interface contract first.

**Continuous Verification — CITATION AUDIT (rotation 1)**:

Queried first 3 facts and audited their reference chains:

- `0097bd2807f0` (economics, provisional): references ["ECON-013"] — ECON-013 is a **symbolic identifier**, not a valid on-chain fact hash. No fact on chain has ID "ECON-013". Reference is unresolvable. **CITATION-001 confirmed persists.**
- `0099d7596f89` (philosophy, provisional): references ["AGRT-001"] — AGRT-001 is similarly a symbolic identifier. No chain fact has this ID. Reference is unresolvable. **CITATION-002 NEW**: `0099d7596f89` references "AGRT-001" — dangling symbolic citation. Same root cause as CITATION-001.
- `00a78e833c1d` (economics, verified): references [] — no citations to audit.

**Root cause of CITATION-001/002**: The chain's reference field stores symbolic short-labels ("ECON-013", "AGRT-001") rather than actual 64-char fact hashes. These cannot be resolved on-chain. The citation system needs a translation layer or must enforce full-hash references at submission time.

**PoT-DESIGN-004 (new)** — Verifier Participation Recording Protocol:

CONF-003/004 reveal that facts verified via challenge_window_end timeout record verifier_count=0 and confidence=N/A. Root cause: passive consensus (no challenges = implicit approval) leaves no verifier footprint.

Design proposal:
- At challenge_window_end, if a fact transitions to verified status with zero explicit challenges, the module should snapshot the active validator set at that block height and record them as **passive verifiers** with weight proportional to their bonded stake.
- This populates verifier_count (= active validator count at verification block) and enables confidence score calculation: `confidence = sum(passive_verifier_stake_weight)`.
- Implementation hook: `claiming_pot/keeper/eligibility.go` items 3-6 are currently deferred. The same EndBlock hook that resolves challenge_window_end should trigger passive verifier recording in the knowledge module.
- Risk: inflates verifier_count artificially. Mitigation: mark passive verifiers with a `passive=true` flag, distinct from active attestors. This preserves integrity while populating the field.

**Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (rotation 1)**:

Advanced PoT-DESIGN-004 (above). Additionally reviewed `claiming_pot/keeper/eligibility.go`: eligibility checks 3-6 (account age, activity score, excluded types, verification level) are all deferred with "always passes" fallback. These are the same checks needed for validator stake-weighted verification. **Action needed**: Gamma to implement activity score eligibility check — this is the most critical deferred item for PoT integrity (prevents Sybil validators from gaining disproportionate influence).
